Formal proof data is scarce and split by prover syntax, foundations, library APIs, and proof styles. Reliable cross-ITP translation could enlarge the usable training pool for learning-based provers and support better autoformalization and proof-repair pipelines. The contribution is not a new model, but a benchmark and verification setup that is closer to real formal ecosystems than type-check-only evaluations.","technical_approach":"ITPEval covers Lean 4, Rocq, Isabelle, and HOL Light, yielding 12 directed translation pairs across dependent type theory and higher-order logic. The benchmark has two tiers. The controlled tier uses self-contained axiomatized files to isolate foundation-level and language-level translation issues. The ecosystem tier uses real library artifacts to expose API mismatches, naming conventions, automation behavior, local helper lemmas, and proof-style differences. The release also includes a unified multi-ITP verification infrastructure that wraps heterogeneous provers behind a common interface and validates outputs with the native target prover. For Lean 4 targets, the paper introduces a Bidirectional Extended Definitional Equivalence check to test whether verified translations preserve theorem meaning rather than merely passing type checking.","experiments":"The benchmark contains 1,560 source files and 6,848 theorems or lemmas. The controlled tier has 64 axiomatized files and 660 lemmas. The ecosystem tier includes 232 Formalizing 100 Theorems files and 1,264 miniF2F statements. The evaluation covers GPT-5.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Qwen3-235B-A22B on both statement and proof translation, with every output checked by the target prover. In the semantic-preservation analysis for miniF2F source-to-Lean 4 statement translations, 174 of 322 verified translations pass BEq, or 54.0%. The remaining 46.0% indicate possible semantic drift or limitations of the restricted equivalence search.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. Read the benchmark design first, especially the split between the controlled and ecosystem tiers. Then inspect the unified verification infrastructure and how it isolates prover environments and reports diagnostics. In the results, focus on translation success by ITP direction and the BEq semantic-equivalence analysis. For foundation-model research, the most reusable pieces are the data organization and semantic verification design, not the ranking of individual closed models.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读 benchmark 分层和 verifier 设计；如果做数学预训练数据，这篇比单一 Lean 榜更能暴露数据迁移成本。","en":"Read the benchmark tiers and verifier design; for math pretraining data, this exposes portability costs better than another single-Lean leaderboard."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jiayi Wu","Robert Joseph George","Anima Anandkumar"],"notable_authors":["Anima Anandkumar"],"tags":["formal-methods","theorem-proving","benchmark","Lean","ITP"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19407","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.19407","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.19949","title":"SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data","abstract":"Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. ho","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-22T09:25:47Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_19949.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CY"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是个人设备助手评测里的一个基础难题：怎样构造既有真实纵向上下文、又能给出可验证标准答案、同时还能共享复现的数据。真实手机轨迹最接近目标场景，但隐私上几乎不可共享；手工写 case 安全但状态稀薄，覆盖不了跨应用、跨时间、多跳检索；LLM 生成 query 或答案虽然快，但如果背后没有完整设备状态，正确性就只能依赖人工或 LLM judge，稳定性和可审计性都不够。SenWorld 的切入点不是继续改 judge，而是把“评测”重写成“可控世界状态生成”问题：只要模拟世界完整拥有设备状态，并且状态演化受物理与外部环境约束，那么很多问题的正确答案就不是事后标注出来的，而是由某一时刻快照中的唯一记录直接决定。","importance":"对大模型基座研究的直接意义不在于训练方法，而在于评测数据生产范式。它给 personal agent / on-device assistant 这类系统提供了一种不依赖 LLM judge 的评测资产构造方式，尤其适合需要长期记忆、跨源检索、时空上下文绑定的任务。对预训练研究员而言，价值主要有三点。第一，它把“ground truth by construction”做到了设备级状态，而不是抽象任务后端，这对未来构造更高可信度的 agent eval 很重要。第二，它展示了 snapshot + pointer label 的标注形式，标签不是自然语言答案，而是指向底层记录的可验证引用，这比开放式文本评分更稳。第三，它说明了高质量评测集未必来自人工标注或模型蒸馏，也可以来自可复现模拟器。量级上，这更像评测基础设施而不是模型方法突破；对通用 LLM 预训练本身启发有限，但对 agent memory、tool use、personal context reasoning 的离线评测设计有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个 physically grounded、deterministic、event-sourced 的 digital twin。论文把 persona、手机、外部服务和物理环境放进同一个随时间推进的世界里统一演化。这里有几个关键设计。第一，物理与外部约束是真实注入的，不是纯文本虚构。世界构建会接入真实地图、天气、节假日、网络等外部数据，让位置、出行、通信、活动安排这些状态变化受现实条件约束。第二，系统是 deterministic 的，同样的初始条件和事件流会得到同样的世界轨迹，这保证了评测可复现。第三，采用 event sourcing 而不是只保留最终数据库状态。也就是每个状态变化都由事件驱动并被记录，系统可以在每个模拟帧导出 full-system snapshot。这样做的好处是，评测样本可以绑定到任意时刻的完整设备状态，而不是只看最终结果。第四，标签机制不是后验生成答案，而是把 query 的正确答案编码成 snapshot 中某个 source table 和 unique record id 的指针。比如“给住在这里的朋友打电话”，正确性最终落在联系人表中某条记录是否与当前位置关联，而不是落在某段自由文本是否看起来合理。第五，论文还设计了 snapshot-centered projection：同一个世界快照可以被确定性地投影成多种下游测试工件，比如用户可见上下文、结构化状态、评测 query 与目标记录等。这使模拟器既是数据生成器，也是评测样本工厂。与已有 state-grounded eval 相比，SenWorld 的区别在于它模拟的是更接近真实 smartphone ecosystem 的完整状态空间，而且评分绕开了 LLM judge。","experiments":"实验主要围绕三件事展开。第一，分布层面的真实性。作者用北京场景下的 16 个 persona 运行整日模拟，并把生成数据与一个保留的真实用户基准在 6 个共享类别上做分布比较。摘要和正文给出的结论是生成分布与真实基准较接近，说明模拟器没有只生成模板化、过于规则的状态组合。第二，行为涌现。论文强调没有为每个 persona 手写 interaction script，而是让行为在环境、日程、设备状态和 persona 设定共同作用下自然出现，进而产生联系人、位置、通信、应用状态之间的长程关联。这一点很关键，因为如果交互脚本是手工编排的，评测集很容易退化成作者预设的有限模式。第三，实用性验证。作者把生成的 evaluation cases 用于诊断一个生产级 smartphone assistant，发现这些 case 能暴露真实缺陷，并帮助定位问题来源。就全文可见内容看，这篇论文更强调评测资产的构造逻辑和案例诊断价值，而不是追求大规模 benchmark 数字。参数量、训练 token、模型提升幅度这类信息并不是重点，因为它不是模型训练论文。若要抓实验重点，建议关注分布对齐图、case 构造示例，以及生产系统缺陷分析部分，而不是期待标准 leaderboard 式结果。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Method，把问题设定吃透，再看实验图表。优先关注三处。第一，SenWorld 的世界状态定义与事件驱动更新机制，这决定了为什么它能给出 by-construction 的标签。第二，snapshot-centered projection 的具体形式，理解“一个快照如何变成多个评测工件”，这是方法最有可迁移性的部分。第三，生产助手缺陷诊断案例，那里最能看出这种评测数据相比人工 case 或 LLM judge 的实际差异。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主线；如果只关心是否值得纳入 agent eval 工具箱，看引言、方法总览图和实验最后一节就够了。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a hard evaluation bottleneck for personal device assistants: how to build context-rich, longitudinal evaluation data with reliable ground truth, without relying on private real-device traces. Real smartphone logs are the natural source, but they are not shareable. Manual case writing is safe but too shallow. LLM-generated cases scale, yet correctness becomes dependent on post-hoc checking or LLM judges. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a fram","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-22T09:40:22Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_19962.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是大推理模型里的 overthinking：模型在已经得到有效推理路径后，仍继续做大量重复验证、回溯和改写，导致输出很长、推理成本高，而且这些额外 token 往往不提升正确率，甚至会引入噪声。论文进一步指出，现有两类做法都不够精细：fast/slow routing 是在推理前做粗粒度二选一，容易误判题目难度；trajectory compression 虽然能缩短链路，但常常把有用的验证步骤和冗余步骤一起压掉，结果是效率上去了，能力却掉了。作者想解决的是一个更细粒度的问题：能不能只删掉重复验证，同时保留甚至增强真正有帮助的推理结构。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不如训练 recipe 或数据论文强，但它对 post-train 与推理行为塑形有现实意义，尤其是 reasoning model 的长度控制、训练信号设计和失败轨迹利用。重要之处有两点。第一，它把 overthinking 视为 reasoning quality 问题，而不只是 decoding 成本问题，因此优化目标不是单纯缩短输出，而是区分“有益验证”和“重复验证”。第二，它没有停在压缩层面，而是试图用失败轨迹里的 from-wrong-to-right 转折去补回甚至提升能力。这一点对后训练很有启发：错误样本不只是负例，也可以是构造高信息密度偏好数据的来源。量级上，这更像 reasoning post-training 方法，而不是基座预训练范式变化；但如果关心 RL/SFT 如何反向塑造模型的思维长度和探索模式，这篇值得看。","technical_approach":"EvoThink 分两阶段。第一阶段是 Self-Pruning Training，目标是去掉冗余验证。模型先对问题生成带 <think>...</think> 的完整推理轨迹和最终答案，然后系统识别 reasoning trajectory 里重复的自我验证、重复求解、对已确认结论的再次展开等片段，并把这些片段剪掉，得到更精炼的轨迹。之后再用这些 pruned trajectories 做 self-training，并且是迭代进行：第 i 轮模型生成，第 i 轮规则或机制剪枝，再训练得到第 i+1 轮模型。这里的关键不是简单按长度截断，而是试图在轨迹内部做结构化删减，所以作者把它称为 fine-grained pruning，而不是 compression。第二阶段是 Aha-Moment Preference Optimization，目标是补能力。作者从错误推理轨迹出发，把失败样本看成一个 population，用 fitness function 评估哪些失败“有潜力”，这里的潜力主要和推理多样性、探索价值有关，而不是只看错得多离谱。然后在这些高潜失败上构造所谓 aha-moment 数据：把错误推理路径与正确答案之间的转折显式组织出来，形成一种 from-wrong-to-right 的偏好对。最后用 DPO 训练模型，让模型偏好能走向新路径、完成关键转折的推理，而不是只学会更短地复述已有正确解。与已有方法相比，EvoThink 的区别在于两点：SPT 不是题目级别的 fast/slow 切换，而是轨迹级别删冗余；AMPO 不是只用正确答案监督，而是把高价值失败转成偏好学习信号，鼓励探索式修正。","experiments":"论文在数学推理和代码生成上做了较系统的实验。引言里给出的一个核心观察是，原始 LRM 超过 65% 的 token 花在重复计算和重复验证上，这为 SPT 提供了动机。实验结论是 EvoThink 能同时改善效率和能力，而不是像很多压缩方法那样用准确率换长度。具体来说，SPT 单独使用时，能显著缩短 reasoning trace，并在多数任务上保持甚至略增性能；加入 AMPO 后，准确率进一步提升，说明从失败到修正的偏好学习确实补回了压缩可能带来的能力损失。论文还做了对比：只用正确解做优化，不如用 aha-moment 数据，因为后者更接近模型真实探索新路径的过程。消融重点通常包括 SPT 的迭代次数、pruning 策略、fitness function 设计，以及是否使用 AMPO。就当前给出的全文片段看，没有完整表格数字可逐项抄录，但主结论很清楚：EvoThink 不是单纯减少 token，而是在更短轨迹下维持或提升数学与代码任务表现。如果精读原文，建议重点核对主表里的长度下降比例、各 benchmark 的准确率变化，以及 AMPO 相对“correct-only preference”基线的增益幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2，对问题定义和两阶段框架建立直觉。然后看 Method 里 SPT 的 pruning 规则或判定机制，判断它到底是在做启发式删句，还是对推理结构有更明确的识别。第二个重点是 AMPO 的 fitness function 和 aha-moment 数据构造方式，这部分决定了它是不是只是另一种偏好数据拼接。实验部分优先看三类结果：长度与准确率的 trade-off、SPT/AMPO 消融、以及只用正确解监督与 aha-moment 监督的对比。预计 25 到 40 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读；如果你做 reasoning post-train，值得把附录里的数据构造细节也看掉。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets overthinking in large reasoning models: after reaching a useful line of reasoning, the model keeps generating repeated verification, re-derivations, and redundant checks. This inflates output length and inference cost, while often adding little or even hurting final accuracy. The authors argue that existing solutions are too coarse. Fast-vs-slow routing makes a pre-reasoning binary decision and can fail on misclassified problems. Trajectory compression shortens outputs, but often removes helpful verification together with redundant verification. The real problem, then, is not just shortening reasoning, but pruning the right parts.","importance":"This is more of a post-training and reasoning-behavior paper than a core pretraining paper, but it is still relevant to base-model researchers interested in how training signals shape reasoning length and exploration. Second, it uses failed trajectories as a source of useful supervision instead of discarding them. That is a meaningful idea for post-train pipelines: failure traces can be converted into high-information preference data, especially when the goal is to encourage productive exploration rather than mere brevity.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"Start with Figure 1 and Figure 2 to understand the problem framing and the two-stage pipeline. pretrain–posttrain 接口高度相关的工作。它把 policy optimization 中经常被经验性讨论的『reward model 饱和/失效』问题形式化成相对分数差距与概率重分配的关系，说明 evaluator 的核心目标不是给出绝对正确分数，而是对当前策略采样集中真正影响更新方向的候选差异保持敏感。对研究 RLHF、RLVR、在线数据筛选、合成数据偏好标注，以及 最关键的不是全局绝对评分，而是在当前候选集内部维持正确、可解释的相对差距。基于这一点，作者提出 DynamicRubric。给定 prompt 和当前策略采样的回答集合，DR-Generator 动态生成面向该集合差异的二元 rubric 条目，并为条目赋权；DR-Verifier 将这些条目逐条应用到每个回答上，输出 yes/no 判断，再聚合成回答级分数。训练时 evaluator 和 policy 交替更新：evaluator 适配新的 response set，policy 用新 evaluator 的分数做优化，从而形成 co-evolution。相比静态 rubric 或固定 reward model，DynamicRubric 的 rubric 是在看到候选回答后生成的，因此能聚焦这一轮优化真正有区分度的错误，例如遗漏约束、单步推理错误或意图不匹配。","experiments":"论文以 Qwen3-8B 作为主实验骨干。训练后的 DR-Generator 在偏好评估任务上超过更大的 evaluator baseline，包括 Qwen3-32B zero-shot 动态 rubric 生成器。将 DynamicRubric 分数用于策略优化时，在开放式生成任务上持续优于 70B reward model 监督和 235B 静态 rubric 监督；在数学、代码、科学问答等可验证推理任务上也有稳定增益。持续 co-evolution 实验显示，随着策略演化，持续更新的 evaluator 比陈旧 evaluator 提供更强监督。论文还报告了在微信搜索 AI 问答场景的大规模线上 A/B：替换原有生产模型后，总搜索量、用户停留时长和正向用户行为均有统计显著提升，并已全量上线。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，但方法部分值得细看。优先读引言和方法论前半部分，尤其是把 score gap 解释为概率重分配信号的那段推导，这是全文最有迁移价值的观点；然后看 Figure 1 理解 DynamicRubric 的生成-验证-策略更新闭环。实验部分重点关注和更大静态 evaluator/reward model 的对比，以及 stale evaluator 与 co-evolved evaluator 的差异，因为这直接支持论文主结论。线上部署结果可作为工业化落地信号参考。预计阅读时间 30–45 分钟。","authors_note":"作者团队来自腾讯微信搜索等相关方向，论文包含微信搜索 AI 问答生产环境部署结果。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"As policies improve during post-training, sampled responses become increasingly similar in overall quality, making it hard for evaluators and reward models to produce reliable relative score gaps. When those gaps collapse or point in the wrong direction, policy optimization receives weak or misleading supervision. The paper addresses how evaluators can co-evolve with the policy so that they remain discriminative on the near-equal candidates produced by the current policy.","importance":"The paper is directly relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface. It formalizes a commonly observed but often informally described problem—reward model saturation or drift—as a question of relative score gaps and probability reallocation. The key message is that the useful quantity for optimization is not an absolute global score, but reliable relative ordering among the candidates that the current policy is actually sampling from. This perspective is useful for research on RLHF, RLVR, online data selection, synthetic preference labeling, and the transition from late-stage pretraining/annealing into post-training.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"The main experiments use Qwen3-8B as the backbone. The trained DR-Generator outperforms larger evaluator baselines on preference evaluation, including a Qwen3-32B zero-shot dynamic rubric generator. When used for policy optimization, DynamicRubric consistently outperforms supervision from a 70B reward model and static-rubric supervision from a 235B rubric generator on open-ended generation, while also yielding consistent gains on math, coding, and scientific QA benchmarks. Continual co-evolution experiments show that an evaluator updated alongside the policy provides stronger supervision than a stale evaluator. The paper also reports large-scale online A/B results in WeChat Search’s AI answering scenario, with statistically significant improvements in total search volume, user duration, and positive user behaviors, leading to full production deployment.","reading_guide":"Skim the paper, but read the method carefully enough to capture the core formalism. The online deployment results are useful as evidence of practical viability. Structural generalization is defined as correct evaluation of a finite set of compositional rules over arbitrary new compositional configurations.","importance":"Its value is mainly theoretical. Benchmarks such as COGS and SLOG test generalization to deeper or unseen syntactic structures, but the target capability is often only operationally specified. The paper turns this into a mathematical object and connects it to circuit complexity. Unbounded tree evaluation corresponds to the Boolean Formula Value Problem, which is NC1-complete. This is then contrasted with TC0-style upper bounds for pure Transformers, giving a formal way to discuss why standard Transformer architectures may struggle with systematic structural generalization.","technical_approach":"The argument starts from two postulates: structure means compositional structure generated by a finite set of n-ary operations, and generalization means correctness beyond any bounded training depth. Expressions are generated recursively from a finite domain M and an operation set Γ, and evaluation proceeds bottom-up along the expression tree. Under a Montagovian instantiation, each rule has a syntactic projection Fγ and a semantic projection Gγ. The semantic side requires tree evaluation, which the paper links to BFVP. The key distinction is between a system that has rules hard-coded, such as a compiler, and a neural model that must recover the capability from finite data.","experiments":"There are no standard empirical experiments. The contribution is a definition and a complexity-theoretic argument. Existing benchmarks are used as motivation: they split train and test sets by derivation depth, but such protocols cannot distinguish rule learning from finite-range behavioral matching. The paper reframes the benchmark target as unbounded compositional evaluation and analyzes the computational requirements of that target.","reading_guide":"A 25-minute skim is appropriate. Ap","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T13:18:12Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_19386.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文问的是：SAE 的 autointerpretability 分数，是否足够稳定，能不能支撑不同 SAE 架构之间的比较。作者关注的不是这些分数是否真的代表“可解释性”，而是一个更基础的问题：同一个特征在不同评测语料、样本抽取、解释模型、提示措辞下，分数是否一致。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在于，它给机制解释和 SAE 评测泼了一盆冷水，但方式是可操作的。很多基座模型分析工作会用 SAE 特征来比较层、模型、训练阶段或架构差异。如果评测分数的方差主要来自 pipeline，而不是被比较对象本身，那么后续关于“哪个 SAE 更可解释”“哪个模型学到了更清晰特征”的结论都需要重新校准。贡献不在新 SAE 架构，而在评测可靠性：它把 autointerpretability 里几个常见自由度拆开，用 ICC 和 Top-k Jaccard 量化其影响，并给出最低报告清单。","technical_approach":"作者把 autointerpretability pipeline 拆成四个方法学变量：评测语料、激活样本的随机抽取、解释器模型、解释文本的改写。架构变量则用不同 SAE 架构表示。评测覆盖四类指标：simulation、detection、fuzzing、purity。simulation 看解释能否预测特征激活；detection 看解释能否帮助判断激活样本；fuzzing 测试解释对干扰样本的辨别能力；purity 衡量高激活样本是否语义集中。模型侧使用 Pythia-160M 和 Apertus-8B Instruct。Pythia 使用 SAEBench 中 layer 8 的 BatchTopK SAE，并加入 Matryoshka SAE 和 Standard SAE 做架构比较；Apertus-8B 上训练了 layer 16、k=160 的 BatchTopK SAE。解释和打分主要用 Gemini Flash，并用 GPT-4o-mini 作为解释器变化条件，打分器固定以隔离 scorer 影响。稳定性用 ICC 衡量分数一致性，用 Top-k Jaccard 衡量被选中特征集合的一致性。这个设计的关键点是把“平均分差异”和“具体特征排序是否稳定”分开看，避免只用均值掩盖 per-feature 层面的不稳定。","experiments":"主要结果有四点。第一，方法学变量带来的总方差超过 SAE 架构差异，在两个源模型和四个指标上都成立。这意味着在没有统一评测设置时，跨论文比较 SAE 架构分数很容易把 pipeline 差异误读为架构差异。第二，不同指标的不稳定来源不同：simulation 和 purity 对随机样本抽取更敏感；detection 对语料选择更敏感；fuzzing 在多种条件下都不可靠。第三，均值稳定不代表特征选择稳定。某些设置下平均分变化不大，但 Top-k 特征集合在不同语料或随机抽样之间接近随机重合，这会影响所有依赖“挑出最可解释特征”的后续实验。第四，解释文本相似度不能诊断分数稳定性。即使不同条件下生成的解释看起来很相似，实际 autointerpretability 分数仍可能有明显波动。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。重点看 Introduction 里的问题定义，Section 2 评测要报告语料、样本抽取、解释器、打分器、prompt 和随机性；只报一个 autointerpretability 均值，信息量不够。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether SAE autointerpretability scores are stable enough to support comparisons across SAE architectures. It does not try to prove whether the scores measure interpretability. It tests a prior requirement: whether the same feature receives consistent scores when the evaluation corpus, sampled activating examples, explainer model, or explanation wording changes.","importance":"The main relevance is to mechanistic interpretability work around pretrained language models. SAE features are increasingly used to compare layers, model families, training stages, and architectures. If most score variance comes from evaluation choices rather than the SAE being evaluated, then claims about more or less interpretable features become fragile. The contribution is an evaluation reliability study rather than a new SAE method. It provides a useful template for reporting and stress-testing autointerpretability pipelines.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"The central finding is that methodological variance exceeds architectural variance across the tested models and metrics. This makes unstandardized cross-paper comparisons of SAE architectures unreliable. The instability profile differs by metric: simulation and purity are especially sensitive to random sampling of examples; detection is more sensitive to corpus choice; fuzzing is unstable across many conditions. Feature rankings are less stable than mean scores suggest. Top-k feature sets selected under one corpus or random draw can have near-chance overlap with those selected under another condition, which matters for any downstream analysis that inspects or intervenes on selected features. The paper also finds that explanation similarity is not a reliable diagnostic: explanations can remain lexically or semantically similar while scores vary substantially.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25–35 minutes. Appendix A is useful for understanding which reporting details are often missing in current SAE evaluations. Appendix B and D matter if reproducing the work, especially for the Apertus-8B SAE setup. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconst","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-22T17:10:23Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_20379.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作检验的是一类近来很受关注的 activation explanation 训练范式：用自然语言自编码器把隐藏状态写成解释，再从解释重建回隐藏状态，并把重建分数当作“解释是否忠实”的证据。论文指出，问题不在某个具体实现，而在目标函数本身：重建只要求解释对激活“足够”，却不惩罚解释里夹带的错误细节。因此，一个解释即使只有大意对、具体事实错，也可能拿到很高分；更糟的是，verbalizer 和 reconstructor 还会共适应出只有彼此懂的私有码，从而让重建分数看起来很好，但解释文本对人并不真实。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，这不是直接改进 LM 训练 recipe 的论文，但它对“如何监督和评估模型内部表征是否可解释”有很强的方法论价值。很多工作默认“可从文本重建激活”就接近 faithful explanation，这篇文章把这个假设拆开了：它证明 reconstruction score 不是 claim-level truth signal。更重要的是，作者给出的正向结论也很清楚——如果真想让某些内容在激活里可被可靠读出，关键不是把 reader 训得更会自圆其说，而是直接在目标模型训练时把这些内容做成可解码的表征。这个结论对 mechanistic interpretability、latent supervision、以及 pretrain/post-train 阶段插入 auxiliary heads 的设计都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法上分三层。第一层是审计工具。作者提出 claim-level flip audit：对解释中的单个具体陈述做最小反事实编辑，只改这一条 claim，再看重建分数下降多少。如果某条 claim 被翻转后几乎不影响重建，说明这条 claim 并没有被激活真正支撑。作者还加入 per-edit validity control，避免因为编辑不自然或改坏句子而污染结论，并用 transform control 证明分数主要跟“gist”走，而不是跟具体措辞绑定。第二层是机制发现。作者在有精确 ground truth 的 synthetic sandbox 中训练 verbalizer/reconstructor，发现标准 recipe 会稳定地产生 private codes：文本里出现对人类来说是错的表述，但 reconstructor 依赖这些表述恢复激活。为识别这种现象，文中用了 grounded-vs-true cross 和 evaluator swap：既看一句话是否影响重建，也看它是否真的对应外部真值；再把共同训练的 evaluator 换成独立 evaluator，检查是否只是配对模型之间的私有协议。第三层是替代方案 RECAP。它不是继续优化 reader，而是在目标模型上加针对外部目标的线性 heads，与模型共同训练，让指定内容在激活中变得 probe-decodable。之后再训练新的 NLA 去 verbalize 这些激活。作者还分析了两个看似自然但失败的方向：一是直接最小化 hidden state 与其重建之间的距离，这会把原本 probe 可解码的内容压掉；二是冻结 probe 监督，但表征漂移后 probe 失效，而这种失效可以被无标签 realignment 逆转，说明问题在 drift，不在内容本身消失。","experiments":"实证最关键的结果有四组。第一，在公开的 Qwen-2.5-7B layer-20 verbalizer/reconstructor 上，解释的重建分数很高，文中给到 floor-normalized centered cosine 约 r~=0.84，但 flip audit 发现只有约 2% 的显著具体 claim 对重建是敏感的。也就是说，系统主要在复述大意，而不是把具体事实说对。第二，在 synthetic sandbox 里，标准共训练 recipe 在两个 domain、5/5 runs 中都学出了 private codes，grounded-minus-true gap 达到 0.30–0.80，说明“影响重建”与“说了真话”可以明显分离。第三，若直接针对 reconstruction test 做修补，效果并不好：重建导向目标会让可探测内容塌缩；奖励 verbalizer grounding 主要是在重排哪些句子更容易被指标计数，而不是提升 claim-level grounding。第四，RECAP 在 sandbox 中 5/5 runs 恢复了指定内容的忠实 verbalization，并迁移到 Pythia-160M；虽然 free-form verbalization 仍不完全，但 designated content 变得可 probe 解码。作者还做了 report-space adversary：攻击者直接优化部署时使用的 reconstruction score，能压掉约 87% 的 lie penalty，几乎骗过该分数；但 RECAP 上的 activation probe 仍能识别谎报，AUC 0.95、recall 0.86，而对照模型 probe 退化到 0.51，接近随机。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看引言和 Figure 1，弄清作者批评的不是某个 verbalizer 写得差，而是 reconstruction-as-faithfulness 这个判据本身有结构性漏洞。第二，看方法部分里 grounding、flip audit、transform control 的定义，理解他们如何把“解释影响重建”与“解释是否真实”拆开。第三，看 synthetic sandbox 和 RECAP 章节，尤其是 private code 的检测方式，以及为什么“训练 reader”不如“训练 target model”。如果时间有限，优先看公开 Qwen 系统的审计结果、private code 的 5/5 复现、以及 adversarial test。预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主线；若要深挖，重点看失败分析部分，因为那里解释了为什么一些直觉上合理的修补会适得其反。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper examines a popular activation-explanation setup: a natural-language autoencoder where a verbalizer turns a hidden state into text, a reconstructor maps the text back to the hidden state, and reconstruction quality is treated as a faithfulness score. The central claim is that this objective is structurally misaligned with claim-level faithfulness. Reconstruction enforces sufficiency, but it does not penalize false additions if they do not change the recovered activation. As a result, explanations can score well while getting specific facts wrong, and co-trained verbalizer/reconstructor pairs can even develop private codes that are useful for reconstruction but not truthful to humans.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for anyone using language to supervise, audit, or read out internal representations. The paper weakens a common assumption: successful activation reconstruction from text is not evidence that the text is faithful at the level of individual claims. The constructive takeaway is also relevant beyond interpretability. If the goal is to make certain content reliably readable from activations, the effective intervention is to train the target model so that the content is explicitly decodable, rather than trying to make a reader model self-justify better. That has implications for auxiliary supervision during pretraining, latent-target design, and the interface between representation learning and post-hoc explanation.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and Figure 1 to understand the target of the critique: not a weak verbalizer, but the reconstruction-based faithfulness criterion itself. Then read the sections defining grounding, the flip audit, and the transform control, since those are the tools that separate 'affects reconstruction' from 'is actually true.' After that, jump to the synthetic sandbox and RECAP sections. The most useful parts are the private-code diagnostics, the evaluator-swap logic, and the failure analysis explaining why reader-side fixes do not work. If time is limited, focus on the Qwen audit, the 5/5 private-code result, and the adversarial test. However, it remains unclear how these structures are stored, selected, and revised. To study this process, we propose thelifted representation hypothesis: LLMs update memory through shared latent structures rather than isolated instance-level facts. This view frames lifting as an efficient use of symmetry across instances, and shattering as the refinement of coarse lifted st","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-02T01:24:33Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_19360.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究的是：LLM在学习规则与例外时，是否倾向于把多个样本压到共享的潜在“类型”表示里，而不是逐条记忆实例；以及当后续出现例外时，模型能否把原先过粗的共享表示重新拆开。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发不在于提出了新的训练方法，而在于它给“模型如何形成抽象、又为何难以修正抽象”提供了一个可操作的分析框架。这个框架把 generalization、memory editing、ICL 更新和 fine-tuning 中的例外学习放到同一视角下：如果样本梯度方向一致，模型更容易形成共享表示；但一旦早期抽象过粗，后续修正就会受到结构性阻力。对做数据配比、课程学习、持续预训练和知识编辑的人，这个视角有参考意义，因为它提示了样本顺序、规则层级和例外密度会影响模型最终形成的是“可细化的抽象”还是“难以打碎的粗类型”。","technical_approach":"作者提出 lifted representation hypothesis：模型会把多个观测映射到共享潜变量 H，再由 H 决定标签或后果，而不是只记 x→y 的实例映射。与普通泛化不同，这里关心的是“哪些样本被归到同一个 H 里”。当后续证据表明这个 H 太粗时，模型需要做 shattering，也就是把原先的共享类型细分成更具体、且仍然后果一致的子类型。\n\n方法上，论文构造了受控的 exception-learning 任务，分别测试两种方向：general-to-specific，即先给一般规则再给例外，看模型能否把已有抽象打碎；specific-to-general，即先给细粒度规则再给更一般的归纳，看模型能否向上提升成共享结构。实验覆盖 in-context learning、LoRA 和 full fine-tuning 三种更新机制。理论上，作者还用梯度对齐解释 lifting 为什么会发生：如果一批样本的梯度内积整体为正，共享更新方向更高效，模型就更倾向于把它们压到同一潜在结构里。这不是严格的机制证明，但给出了一个和训练动力学相连的解释。","experiments":"核心实验结论是：模型更擅长 bottom-up lifting，不擅长 top-down shattering。也就是，先看到若干具体规则，再归纳成更一般结构，通常比“先学到一个大类，再在后面加入例外并重分类型”更容易。这个现象在 ICL、LoRA、全参微调三种设定下都出现，说明它不像某一种训练范式的偶然产物，更像共享表示修正本身的难点。论文还讨论了嵌套规则和例外层级更深时，shattering failure 会更明显。若想快速把握结果，重点看不同暴露顺序下的准确率对比，以及作者如何区分“没见够例子”与“已有抽象难以重写”这两种失败来源。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读。先看 Introduction 和 Methods，把 lifting / shattering 的定义吃透，再看实验设计图，理解 general-to-specific 与 specific-to-general whether LLMs tend to store observations through shared latent rule-like structures rather than isolated instance memories, and whether they can later refine an overly coarse shared structure when exceptions appear.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is conceptual rather than algorithmic. It offers a useful lens on how abstraction forms and why abstraction revision can fail. The key idea is that learning may favor shared latent structures when examples have aligned gradients, but those same shared structures become hard to revise once exceptions arrive. That has implications for curriculum design, continual pretraining, data ordering, and model editing: the final representation may depend not only on what data the model sees, but also on whether the data encourages a coarse shared type that later resists refinement.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"The main empirical result is asymmetric behavior: models are better at bottom-up lifting than top-down shattering. In other words, they more reliably build a broader abstraction from specific examples than revise an existing broad abstraction after exceptions are introduced. This pattern appears across in-context learning, LoRA, and full fine-tuning, which suggests that the difficulty is not specific to one adaptation method.\n\nThe paper further shows that shattering failures become more pronounced when the rule structure is nested or when exceptions require finer-grained retyping. A useful part of the experimental design is that it tries to separate two failure modes: lack of evidence versus inability to revise a shared internal structure. For a quick read, focus on the accuracy comparisons under different exposure orders and on the analyses that support this distinction.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper.
